Assistant2026-01-10T20:31:16.537Z
Thinking

The k8s bootstrap is still failing with the same issue - it times out on the first attempt (after 1m35s), and then retry attempts fail because ports 2379/2380 are still in use.

The cleanup steps we added (snap remove --purge k8s and fuser -k) aren't working because: 1. The k8s snap doesn't exist yet when we run them (before concierge installs it) 2. After the first bootstrap attempt fails, concierge's retry logic is encountering the bound ports

The issue is that k8s bootstrap is failing with "context canceled" during post-bootstrap actions. This suggests it's timing out or encountering some other issue in GitHub Actions.

Looking at the ubuntu-manpages-operator, they use self-hosted runners for integration tests, not GitHub-hosted runners. That's probably why their k8s bootstrap works.

I think we need to take a different approach. Since k8s bootstrap is unreliable in GitHub Actions, we should either: 1. Skip the k8s setup and just use LXD with juju for testing (change from dev preset to machine preset) 2. Try to make the k8s bootstrap more robust with better timeout handling

Actually, looking at the charm - it's a Kubernetes charm (beszel-k8s-operator), so we need k8s, not machine charms. But maybe we don't need the full dev preset with both LXD and k8s - we just need k8s.

Let me check what concierge presets are available and see if there's one that's more suitable.

Actually, thinking about this more - the issue is that concierge's k8s bootstrap is timing out. The error "context canceled" suggests it's hitting some timeout during the post-bootstrap actions.

One option: add a longer timeout or make the retry logic more robust. But we don't control concierge's internals.

Another option: Don't use concierge for k8s setup. Instead, manually bootstrap k8s with more control over the timeout and retry logic.

Could try microk8s as an alternative backend instead of the k8s snap, since concierge might have better support for it. The ubuntu-manpages-operator uses -p machine in their spread.yaml because they're testing a machine charm, but for a k8s charm we'd need a k8s controller. I need to reconsider the architecture - the charm is for Kubernetes, the integration tests deploy it to a Juju model, and the model needs a k8s backend to run properly.

Concierge's dev preset should handle both LXD and k8s setup, but the k8s bootstrap keeps timing out in CI. Rather than fighting the flakiness, I could add a retry mechanism with cleanup between attempts - detect when bootstrap fails, clear the bound ports, and try again. A simple sleep between retries might give the processes time to fully release their ports before the next attempt.

Assistant2026-01-10T20:31:16.584Z

The k8s bootstrap is still timing out and leaving ports bound. The cleanup we added runs before concierge, so it doesn't help with the retry failures. Let me add a retry loop that cleans up after each failed attempt:

Assistant2026-01-10T20:31:20.194Z
✏️ Edit task.yaml
/home/ubuntu/beszel-operator/spread/deploy/basic/task.yaml
−
prepare: |
  # Remove Docker containerd directory if it exists (conflicts with k8s snap)
  sudo rm -rf /run/containerd

  # Remove any existing k8s installation to ensure clean state
  sudo snap remove --purge k8s 2>/dev/null || true

  # Kill any processes on k8s/etcd ports to ensure clean state
  sudo fuser -k 2379/tcp 2380/tcp 6443/tcp 10250/tcp 2>/dev/null || true

  # Setup Juju environment with concierge
  concierge prepare --trace -p dev
+
prepare: |
  # Remove Docker containerd directory if it exists (conflicts with k8s snap)
  sudo rm -rf /run/containerd

  # Retry loop for concierge prepare (k8s bootstrap can be flaky)
  max_attempts=3
  attempt=1
  while [ $attempt -le $max_attempts ]; do
    echo "Attempt $attempt of $max_attempts: Running concierge prepare..."

    if concierge prepare --trace -p dev; then
      echo "Concierge prepare succeeded!"
      break
    else
      echo "Concierge prepare failed on attempt $attempt"

      if [ $attempt -lt $max_attempts ]; then
        echo "Cleaning up for retry..."
        # Kill processes on k8s/etcd ports
        sudo fuser -k 2379/tcp 2380/tcp 6443/tcp 10250/tcp 2>/dev/null || true
        # Remove k8s snap to ensure clean state
        sudo snap remove --purge k8s 2>/dev/null || true
        # Wait for cleanup to complete
        sleep 5
      else
        echo "All attempts failed"
        exit 1
      fi
    fi

    attempt=$((attempt + 1))
  done

Good! Now I've added a retry loop that: 1. Tries concierge prepare up to 3 times 2. If it fails, kills processes on k8s ports and removes the k8s snap 3. Waits 5 seconds for cleanup to complete 4. Retries

fix: add retry loop for concierge prepare with cleanup

k8s bootstrap can timeout in GitHub Actions. Add a retry loop that:
- Attempts concierge prepare up to 3 times
- After each failure, kills processes on k8s/etcd ports
- Removes k8s snap to ensure clean state for retry
- Waits 5 seconds between attempts

Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 4.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
EOF
)"

Summary:


1. Primary Request and Intent:


The user requested help getting the CI tests passing for the tonyandrewmeyer/beszel-k8s-operator repository. They had installed gh CLI tool and needed the integration tests to work. A critical constraint was that the solution MUST use charmcraft test infrastructure (later clarified to be charmcraft.spread). The user pointed to https://github.com/canonical/ubuntu-manpages-operator/ as a reference implementation.


2. Key Technical Concepts:


    

  • charmcraft.spread: Tool for running spread-based integration tests for Juju charms
    • 

  • Spread: Testing framework that can run tests in various backends (LXD, adhoc, craft)
    • 

  • github-ci backend: Adhoc spread backend that runs tests directly on GitHub Actions host via SSH
    • 

  • Concierge: Tool that sets up Juju and Kubernetes environments for testing
    • 

  • LXD networking issues: Containers in GitHub Actions cannot get network connectivity
    • 

  • k8s bootstrap flakiness: Kubernetes snap bootstrap can timeout in CI, leaving ports bound
    • 

  • Snap confinement: Juju snap requires files to be in /root directory
    • 

  • canonical/setup-lxd: GitHub Action for setting up LXD properly
    • 



3. Files and Code Sections:


spread.yaml


Why important: Defines how spread tests run in different environments
Changes made: 
- Fixed syntax error (removed trailing colon from ubuntu-24.04:)
- Removed craft backend (not supported by charmcraft.spread)
- Added github-ci adhoc backend for running on GitHub Actions host
- Set path: /root/proj for snap confinement compatibility
- Added environment variable for CI detection


Final state:


project: beszel-operator

backends:
  github-ci:
    type: adhoc
    manual: true
    allocate: |
      sudo sed -i "s|#PasswordAuthentication yes|PasswordAuthentication yes|g" /etc/ssh/sshd_config
      sudo sed -i "s|KbdInteractiveAuthentication no|KbdInteractiveAuthentication yes|g" /etc/ssh/sshd_config
      sudo rm -f /etc/ssh/sshd_config.d/60-cloudimg-settings.conf /etc/ssh/sshd_config.d/50-cloud-init.conf
      sudo systemctl daemon-reload
      sudo systemctl restart ssh

      sudo useradd spread -s /bin/bash -m
      echo "spread:$SPREAD_PASSWORD" | sudo chpasswd
      echo 'spread ALL=(ALL) NOPASSWD:ALL ' | sudo tee /etc/sudoers.d/99-spread-user

      ADDRESS "127.0.0.1"
    discard: |
      sudo userdel -f -r spread || true
      sudo rm -f /etc/sudoers.d/99-spread-user

    systems:
      - ubuntu-24.04:
          username: spread
          workers: 1

environment:
  CI: "$(HOST: echo ${CI:-})"

suites:
  spread/deploy/:
    summary: Deployment tests

exclude:
  - .git
  - .coverage
  - .github
  - .pytest_cache
  - .ruff_cache
  - .tox
  - venv

path: /root/proj

kill-timeout: 1h



.github/workflows/ci.yaml


Why important: Defines the CI pipeline
Changes made: 
- Changed from charmcraft test to charmcraft.spread
- Removed Concierge setup from CI (moved to spread task)
- Added canonical/setup-lxd action
- Changed runner to ubuntu-24.04
- Removed Docker removal (was causing issues)


Final state for integration job:


integration:
  name: Integration Tests
  runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
  permissions:
    content: read
  steps:
    - name: Checkout
      uses: actions/checkout@v6
      with:
        persist-credentials: false

    - name: Setup LXD
      uses: canonical/setup-lxd@8c6a87bfb56aa48f3fb9b830baa18562d8bfd4ee

    - name: Install charmcraft
      run: sudo snap install charmcraft --classic

    - name: Install concierge
      run: sudo snap install concierge --classic

    - name: Run integration tests via spread
      run: charmcraft.spread -v github-ci:ubuntu-24.04:spread/deploy/



spread/deploy/basic/task.yaml


Why important: Defines the actual test execution
Changes made:
- Added retry loop (up to 3 attempts) for concierge prepare
- Clean up /run/containerd before concierge
- Kill processes on k8s/etcd ports between retries
- Fixed PROJECT_PATH → SPREAD_PATH
- Use concierge to set up Juju environment
- Pack charm if needed
- Run pytest with Jubilant


Final prepare section:


prepare: |
  # Remove Docker containerd directory if it exists (conflicts with k8s snap)
  sudo rm -rf /run/containerd

  # Retry loop for concierge prepare (k8s bootstrap can be flaky)
  max_attempts=3
  attempt=1
  while [ $attempt -le $max_attempts ]; do
    echo "Attempt $attempt of $max_attempts: Running concierge prepare..."

    if concierge prepare --trace -p dev; then
      echo "Concierge prepare succeeded!"
      break
    else
      echo "Concierge prepare failed on attempt $attempt"

      if [ $attempt -lt $max_attempts ]; then
        echo "Cleaning up for retry..."
        # Kill processes on k8s/etcd ports
        sudo fuser -k 2379/tcp 2380/tcp 6443/tcp 10250/tcp 2>/dev/null || true
        # Remove k8s snap to ensure clean state
        sudo snap remove --purge k8s 2>/dev/null || true
        # Wait for cleanup to complete
        sleep 5
      else
        echo "All attempts failed"
        exit 1
      fi
    fi

    attempt=$((attempt + 1))
  done

  # Add a Juju model for testing
  juju add-model "${MODEL}"

  # Install uv for Python dependency management
  snap install --classic astral-uv

  # Set up uv and install test dependencies
  cd "${SPREAD_PATH}"
  uv sync --group integration

  # Pack the charm if not already packed
  if [[ ! -f "${SPREAD_PATH}"/*.charm ]]; then
    charmcraft pack
  fi

  # Ensure charm is owned by the current user
  chown "$(id -u):$(id -g)" "${SPREAD_PATH}"/*.charm



.gitignore


Why important: Prevent committing temporary spread files
Changes made: Added .craft-spread-*/ and .spread-reuse.*.yaml


spread/.extension


Why important: Enables craft backend support in spread (though we ended up not using it)
Changes made: Added craft backend extension code (from charmcraft)


4. Errors and Fixes:


Error 1: "nothing matches provider filter"


Cause: Trailing colon in spread.yaml (ubuntu-24.04: instead of ubuntu-24.04)
Fix: Removed the trailing colon
Location: spread.yaml line 7


Error 2: LXD networking failure in GitHub Actions


Cause: LXD containers cannot get network connectivity in GitHub Actions (ping to 8.8.8.8 failed)
Initial approach: Tried to fix LXD networking with IP forwarding, DNS config
User feedback: "no, it is very important that we use charmcraft test"
Final fix: Switched to charmcraft.spread with github-ci backend (runs on host, not in containers)


Error 3: "backend 'craft' has unsupported type 'craft'"


Cause: charmcraft.spread doesn't support the craft backend type
Fix: Removed craft backend from spread.yaml


Error 4: "PROJECT_PATH: unbound variable"


Cause: Used wrong environment variable name
Fix: Changed all PROJECT_PATH references to SPREAD_PATH in task.yaml


Error 5: k8s bootstrap timeout and port conflicts


Cause: k8s snap bootstrap times out (~1m35s), leaves ports 2379/2380 bound, causing retries to fail
Fix: Added retry loop (up to 3 attempts) that:
  - Removes /run/containerd before first attempt
  - Kills processes on k8s/etcd ports after failed attempts
  - Removes k8s snap completely between attempts
  - Waits 5 seconds for cleanup
Result: Concierge successfully prepared on second attempt in CI


Error 6: "/run/containerd already exists"


Cause: Docker removal leaves /run/containerd directory, k8s snap needs this path
Fix: Remove /run/containerd in spread task before running concierge

    19→    else
    20→      echo "Concierge prepare failed on attempt $attempt"
    21→
    22→      if [ $attempt -lt $max_attempts ]; then
    23→        echo "Cleaning up for retry..."
    24→        # Kill processes on k8s/etcd ports
    25→        sudo fuser -k 2379/tcp 2380/tcp 6443/tcp 10250/tcp 2>/dev/null || true
    26→        # Remove k8s snap to ensure clean state
    27→        sudo snap remove --purge k8s 2>/dev/null || true
    28→        # Wait for cleanup to complete
    29→        sleep 5
    30→      else
    31→        echo "All attempts failed"
    32→        exit 1
    33→      fi
    34→    fi
    35→
    36→    attempt=$((attempt + 1))
    37→  done
    38→
    39→  # Add a Juju model for testing
    40→  juju add-model "${MODEL}"
    41→
    42→  # Install uv for Python dependency management
    43→  snap install --classic astral-uv
    44→
    45→  # Set up uv and install test dependencies
    46→  cd "${SPREAD_PATH}"
    47→  uv sync --group integration
    48→
    49→  # Pack the charm if not already packed
    50→  if [[ ! -f "${SPREAD_PATH}"/*.charm ]]; then
    51→    charmcraft pack
    52→  fi
    53→
    54→  # Ensure charm is owned by the current user
    55→  chown "$(id -u):$(id -g)" "${SPREAD_PATH}"/*.charm
    56→
    57→execute: |
    58→  cd "${SPREAD_PATH}"
    59→
    60→  # Find the charm file
    61→  CHARM_PATH="$(ls "${SPREAD_PATH}"/*.charm | head -1)"
    62→  export CHARM_PATH
    63→
    64→  # Run integration tests using pytest with Jubilant
    65→  uv run pytest tests/integration -v --tb=short
    66→
    67→restore: |
    68→  # Clean up the Juju model
    69→  juju destroy-model --no-prompt --force --destroy-storage --no-wait --timeout=60s "${MODEL}" || true
    70→
    71→  # Restore concierge environment if not in CI
    72→  if [[ -z "${CI:-}" ]]; then
    73→    concierge restore --trace || true
    74→  fi
    75→

Based on standard concierge usage, the profiles typically are:- dev - LXD for machine charms
- microk8s - MicroK8s for Kubernetes charms


So I should change -p dev to -p microk8s. Then the model should be added to the microk8s cloud/controller.

        exit 1

        exit 1

Assistant2026-01-10T21:37:25.545Z


Thinking
Great! Now let me provide a summary for the user. The CI tests are now fully passing. The key issue was that we were using the wrong concierge profile for a Kubernetes charm.


Assistant2026-01-10T21:37:30.036Z


Perfect! I've committed the CHANGELOG update. Here's a summary of what we accomplished:


Summary


Goal: Get CI tests passing for the beszel-k8s-operator repository.


Root Cause


The charm is a Kubernetes charm (has containers: and OCI resources:), but the integration tests were using concierge prepare -p dev which sets up an LXD environment for machine charms. This caused the charm to be deployed on an LXD cloud where it was stuck in "Waiting for Pebble" status because Pebble only works in Kubernetes pods.


Solution


Changed the spread test configuration to use concierge prepare -p microk8s instead of -p dev, which properly sets up a MicroK8s Kubernetes environment for testing Kubernetes charms.


Results


✅ All CI tests now pass:
- Lint: 14s
- Unit Tests: 32s

- Integration Tests: 10m33s


Changes Made


    

  1. spread/deploy/basic/task.yaml: Changed from dev profile to microk8s profile
    2. 

  2. CHANGELOG.md: Documented the fix
    3. 



The CI is now fully functional and ready for ongoing development!
